Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The waves operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 346. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 621 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on waves structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 346. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 621. QED.
